    function loadScript(src, callback) {
        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.src = src;
        script.defer = !0;
        script.onload = callback;
        document.head.appendChild(script)
    }

    function handleCredentialResponse(response) {
        console.log("Encoded JWT ID token: " + response.credential);
        // You can decode the JWT to get user info
        const idToken = response.credential;
        sendTokenToServer(idToken);
    }

    function sendTokenToServer(token) {
        fetch('https://www.research360.in/auth.php', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ token: token })
        })
            .then(response => {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Network response was not ok ' + response.statusText);
                }
                window.location.href = 'https://www.research360.in/login.php?forcesocial=1';
                //return response.json();
            })
            .then(data => console.log(data))
            .catch(error => console.error('Error:', error));
    }


    
        function loginPopup(page, id = "") {
            event.preventDefault();
            $.ajax({
                type: "post",
                url: "https://www.research360.in/ajax/loginPopup.php",
                data: {
                    loginPopup: 1,
                    page: page
                }
            }).done(function (data) {
                if (page == "advisoryplan") {
                    $("#logincontent").html("Please Log in to continue with your subscription.");
                } else {
                    $("#logincontent").html("Please log in to continue and explore this feature.");

                }
                if (page == "") {
                    $('.red_link').val("mo-advice");
                } else {
                    $('.red_link').val(page);
                }
                $("#planParam").val(id);
                openPopup('firstPopup');
                return !1;
            });
        }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        if (!isMobile()) {
            document.addEventListener('click', function () {
                var pol = document.getElementById('pol');
                if (pol && pol.style.display === "block") {
                    pol.style.display = 'none'
                }
                document.getElementById("myDropdown-search").style.display = "none";
                document.getElementById("usr").value = ""
            });
            let myfuncMenu = document.getElementById('myfunc');
            if (myfuncMenu) {
                myfuncMenu.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    var pol = document.getElementById('pol');
                    if (pol.style.display === "none") {
                        pol.style.display = 'block'
                    } else {
                        pol.style.display = 'none'
                    }
                    event.stopPropagation()
                })
            }
        }
        if (isMobile()) {
            let overlay = document.querySelector('.overlay');
            let menu = document.getElementById('myfunc1');
            if (overlay) {
                overlay.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    document.querySelector('.mobileMenu').classList.toggle('open');
                    document.querySelector('.overlay').classList.toggle('open');
                    document.querySelector('.overlay').style.display = 'none'
                })
            }
            if (menu) {
                menu.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    document.querySelector('.mobileMenu').classList.toggle('open');
                    document.querySelector('.overlay').classList.toggle('open');
                    document.querySelector('.mobileMenu').style.display = 'block';
                    if (document.querySelector('.overlay').style.display === "block") {
                        document.querySelector('.overlay').style.display = 'none'
                    } else {
                        document.querySelector('.overlay').style.display = 'block'
                    }
                    document.body.classList.toggle('no-scroll');
                })
            }
        }

        // }
        document.getElementById('signInTab').addEventListener('click', function () {

            document.getElementById('signInForm').classList.add('active');

            document.getElementById('signUpForm').classList.remove('active');
            this.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('signUpTab').classList.remove('active');
        });

        document.getElementById('signUpTab').addEventListener('click', function () {

            document.getElementById('signInForm').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('signUpForm').classList.add('active');
            this.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('signInTab').classList.remove('active');
        });
    });

    function cleanSpecharwithHyphen(string) {
        if (string) {
            var string = string.toLowerCase().replaceAll(" ", "-");
            return string.toLowerCase().replaceAll("--", "-").replace(/[^\w -]/g, "")
        } else {
            return ""
        }
    }

    function moveFocus(event, nextElementId, prevElementId) {
        // Check if Backspace is pressed
        console.log(event);
        if (event.key === "Backspace") {
            // If the current input is empty, move focus to the previous element
            if (event.target.value.length === 0) {
                const prevElement = document.getElementById(prevElementId);
                if (prevElement) {
                    prevElement.focus();
                }
            }
        } else if (event.target.value.length === 1) {
            // If exactly one character is entered, move focus to the next element
            const nextElement = document.getElementById(nextElementId);
            if (nextElement) {
                nextElement.focus();
            }
        }
    }


    function timeConvert(unix_timestamp) {
        var date = new Date(unix_timestamp * 1000);
        var hours = date.getHours();
        var minutes = "0" + date.getMinutes();
        var seconds = "0" + date.getSeconds();
        var formattedTime = hours + ':' + minutes.substr(-2);
        return formattedTime;
    }


    function timehours(params) {
        var date = new Date(params);
        var day = date.getDate();
        var month = date.toLocaleString('default', {
            month: 'short'
        });
        var year = date.getFullYear();
        var formattedDate = day + ' ' + month + ', ' + year;
        var hours = date.getHours();
        var minutes = date.getMinutes();
        var ampm = hours >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
        hours = hours % 12;
        hours = hours ? hours : 12;
        var formattedTime = hours.toString().padStart(2, '0') + ':' + minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0') + ' ' + ampm;
        return formattedDate + " | " + formattedTime
    }

    function SearchStocks() {
        const input = document.getElementById("usr");
        if (input.value.length >= 3) {
            var searchData = {
                "searchKeyword": input.value
            };
            fetch(base_url_web + 'common/search/searchApi.php', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'text/html'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(searchData)
            }).then(response => {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Network response was not ok')
                }
                return response.text()
            }).then(data => {
                document.getElementById("myDropdown-search").innerHTML = data;
                document.getElementById("myDropdown-search").style.display = "block"
            }).catch(error => {
                console.error('There was a problem with the fetch operation:', error)
            })
        } else {
            document.getElementById("myDropdown-search").style.display = "none";
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
        }
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        const topnav = document.querySelector(".topnav");
        if (!event.target.closest('.searchicon') && !event.target.closest('#usr') && topnav.classList.contains("open")) {
            document.querySelector('.searchicon').click();
            document.querySelector('#myDropdown-search').style.display = 'none';
            const usrField = document.querySelector('#usr');
            if (usrField) {
                usrField.value = ''
            }
        }
    });


    document.querySelector('.searchicon').addEventListener('click', function () {
        document.querySelector('.topnav').classList.toggle('open');
        document.querySelector('.backdrop').classList.toggle('active');
        document.getElementById('usr').focus();
    });
    document.querySelector('.backdrop').addEventListener('click', function () {
        document.querySelector('.topnav').classList.toggle('open');
        document.querySelector('.backdrop').classList.toggle('active');
    });

     function closeThirdPopup(popupId) {
        document.getElementById('thirdPopup').style.display = 'none';
    }
    function closeSevenPopup(popupId) {
        // if(popupId=='seventhPopup' || popupId=='thirdPopup' )
        document.getElementById('seventhPopup').style.display = 'none';
    }

     function encoded(str) {
        if (str) {
            var encoded = "";
            str = btoa(str);
            str = btoa(str);
            for (i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
                var a = str.charCodeAt(i);
                var b = a ^ 10; // bitwise XOR with any number, e.g. 123
                encoded = encoded + String.fromCharCode(b);
            }
            encoded = btoa(encoded);
            return encoded;
        } else {
            return "";
        }
    }

                        It looks like your KYC is not yet done. We kindly request you to open a Demat account with us to complete the process.
                    </p>
                    <div class="mtop20"></div>
                    <a href="javascript:void(0);" class="subscribecta semibold" onclick="window.open('https://www.motilaloswal.com/open-demat-account/', '_blank');" data-bs-dismiss="modal">Proceed</a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- emudraCompleted Modal (Custom styling to match kraCompleteModal) -->
<div id="emudraCompleted" style="display:none; position:fixed; z-index:9999; left:0; top:0; width:100vw; height:100vh; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.4); justify-content:center; align-items:center;">
  <div style="background:#fff; border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.18); max-width:420px; width:90vw; padding:32px 24px 24px 24px; position:relative; text-align:center;">
    <button onclick="closeEmudraCompleted()" style="position:absolute; top:18px; right:18px; background:none; border:none; font-size:22px; color:#222; cursor:pointer;">&times;</button>
    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; align-items:center; margin-bottom:18px;">
      <div style="background:#32248e; border-radius:50%; width:64px; height:64px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;">
        <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="12" fill="none"/>
          <path d="M7 13.5L10.5 17L17 10.5" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
        </svg>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:1.35rem; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:10px; color:#222;">KYC Successful</div>
    <div class="kycCompleteMsg">Great! You have successfully completed your KYC Process.</div>
    <button onclick="closeEmudraCompleted(); cleverTapAdvisory(cleverTapData); window.location.href='https://www.research360.in/subscription-plans';" style="width:100%; background:#32248e; color:#fff; font-weight:600; font-size:1.05rem; border:none; border-radius:8px; padding:13px 0; cursor:pointer; transition:background 0.2s;">Done</button>
  </div>
</div>




<div class="modal fade" id="emudraFailed" tabindex="-1" aria-labelledby="emudraFailed" aria-hidden="true"
    data-bs-backdrop="static">
    <div class="modal-dialog">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <div class="modal-body" style="padding: 32px 24px; border-radius: 12px; background: #fff; position: relative;">
                <button type="button" class="btn-close rightpull" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close" style="position: absolute; right: 16px; top: 16px;"></button>
                <div class="d-flex flex-column align-items-center justify-content-center text-center">
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 18px;">
                        <img src="https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/dist/images/error-illustration.svg" alt="Error" width="80" height="80" loading="lazy" style="margin-bottom: 10px;" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='https://www.research360.in/Assets/dist/images/something-wrong.png';">
                    </div>
                    <div class="fw-bold" style="font-size: 22px; margin-bottom: 12px;">Uh-oh!<br>Something went wrong.</div>
                    <div style="color: #444; font-size: 15px; margin-bottom: 32px; max-width: 320px;">Your request could not be completed at the moment.<br>Kindly retry after some time.</div>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary w-100" style="max-width: 320px; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 500; font-size: 16px; padding: 10px 0; background: #2E2A94; border: none;" data-bs-dismiss="modal" onclick="window.location.href='https://www.research360.in/subscription-plans'; return false;">Try Again</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- Add a full-screen overlay for loader -->
<div id="loaderkyc-overlay" style="display:none;position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100vw;height:100vh;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.7);z-index:9999;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">
    <div class="mt-3 loading-text">
        <img src="https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/dist/images/loading.gif" width="40px" height="40px" loading="lazy">
    </div>
</div>

<!-- KRA Success Modal (No Bootstrap) -->
<div id="kraCompleteModal" style="display:none; position:fixed; z-index:9999; left:0; top:0; width:100vw; height:100vh; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.4); justify-content:center; align-items:center;">
  <div style="background:#fff; border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.18); max-width:420px; width:90vw; padding:32px 24px 24px 24px; position:relative; text-align:center;">
    <button onclick="kraProceedAction()" style="position:absolute; top:18px; right:18px; background:none; border:none; font-size:22px; color:#222; cursor:pointer;">&times;</button>
    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; align-items:center; margin-bottom:18px;">
      <div style="background:#32248e; border-radius:50%; width:64px; height:64px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;">
        <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="12" fill="none"/>
          <path d="M7 13.5L10.5 17L17 10.5" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
        </svg>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:1.35rem; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:10px; color:#222;">KRA Success!</div>
    <div class="kraCompleteMsg">Since your KYC is already done, we are now redirecting you to<br>complete the Aadhaar e-sign process.</div>
    <button class="proceedemudra" style="width:100%; background:#32248e; color:#fff; font-weight:600; font-size:1.05rem; border:none; border-radius:8px; padding:13px 0; cursor:pointer; transition:background 0.2s;" onclick="kraProceedAction();cleverTapCall(`Redirect_dematopening`,`withoutText`);">Proceed</button>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
// Modal management functions
function showKraCompleteModal() {
  hideAllModals();
  document.getElementById('kraCompleteModal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function kraProceedAction() {
  document.getElementById('kraCompleteModal').style.display = 'none';
  removeModalBackdrop();
}

function showEmudraCompleted() {
  hideAllModals();
  document.getElementById('emudraCompleted').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeEmudraCompleted() {
  document.getElementById('emudraCompleted').style.display = 'none';
  removeModalBackdrop();
  var specialKycCouponApplied = localStorage.getItem('specialKycCouponApplied');
  if(specialKycCouponApplied === 'true') {
    localStorage.removeItem('specialKycCouponApplied');
    
  }
}

function hideAllModals() {
  // Hide all modals and remove any existing backdrops
  document.getElementById('kraCompleteModal').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('emudraCompleted').style.display = 'none';
  removeModalBackdrop();
}

function removeModalBackdrop() {
  // Remove any Bootstrap modal backdrops that might be lingering
  const backdrops = document.querySelectorAll('.modal-backdrop');
  backdrops.forEach(backdrop => backdrop.remove());
  
  // Remove modal-open class from body
  document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
  document.body.style.overflow = '';
  document.body.style.paddingRight = '';
}

// Close modal when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  if (e.target.id === 'kraCompleteModal') {
    document.getElementById('kraCompleteModal').style.display = 'none';
    removeModalBackdrop();
  }
  if (e.target.id === 'emudraCompleted') {
    closeEmudraCompleted();
  }
});

// Handle Bootstrap modal events to ensure proper cleanup
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  // Listen for Bootstrap modal hide events
  const bootstrapModals = ['#eMudraProcess', '#errorkycpopup', '#openDematAccountModal', '#emudraFailed', '#emudraNotComplete'];
  
  bootstrapModals.forEach(function(modalId) {
    const modalElement = document.querySelector(modalId);
    if (modalElement) {
      modalElement.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function() {
        setTimeout(removeModalBackdrop, 100);
      });
    }
  });
});
</script>
<!-- End KRA Success Modal -->

<script type="text/javascript">
    var emudraRedirectUrl = null; // Store emudraurl for redirect
    function loadScript(src, callback) {
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.src = src;
            script.defer = !0;
            script.onload = callback;
            document.head.appendChild(script)
    }
    function checkcountryEMudra(tryAgain=null) {
        // alert('1');
        loadScript('https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/js/jquery-3.6.0.min.js', function() {
        loadScript('https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/dist/js/flatpickr.min.js', function() {
        loadScript('https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/dist/js/bundle.min.js', function() {
        loadScript('https://d1tymi9mhi46bx.cloudfront.net/Assets/uat/dist/js/crypto-js4.1.1.min.js', function() {
        // var cleverTapData = {
        //     'eventName': "KYC_VerifyKYCPage",
        //     'date': '2026-04-30 13:04:21',
        //     'keyName': 'KYC Source',
        //     'keyVal': 'Web'
        // }
        // cleverTapEventCall(cleverTapData);

        // alert('1');
        $('#dobEmudra').flatpickr({
            dateFormat: "d/m/Y", // Date format (YYYY-MM-DD)
        });

       
        $.ajax({
            url: 'https://www.research360.in/ajax/mo-advice/getStateCityApiHandler.php',
            type: 'POST',
            data: {
                userid: "",
                flag: 'checkcountry'
            },
            success: function (responce) {
                var responseObject = JSON.parse(responce);
                console.log('checkcountry flag response');
                console.log(responseObject);
                if(responseObject.kra_status == "N" &&  responseObject.panname != ""){
                    $('#panEmudraValid').val(responseObject.panno);
                    $('#dobEmudra').val(responseObject.dob);
                    $('#nameEmudra').val(responseObject.panname);
                    $('#kycname').show();
                    $("#eMudraProcess").modal('show');
                    $("#emudrasub").html('Update');
                    if(tryAgain=='' || tryAgain==null){
                        $("#openDematAccountModal").modal('show');
                    }
                    
                }else{
                    $("#eMudraProcess").modal('show');
                    $("#emudrasub").show();
                    $("#viewdetails_payment1").modal('hide');
                    
                }
                if (responseObject.country == '' || responseObject.country == null) {
                    if (responseObject.country == '' || responseObject.country == null) {
                        $("#emudracountry").html('<option value="">Select Country</option><option value="India" selected>India</option><option value="OutsideIndia">Outside India</option>');
                        console.log('in country selection');
                        $.ajax({
                            url: 'https://www.research360.in/ajax/mo-advice/getStateCityApiHandler.php', // Change this to your API endpoint
                            type: 'POST',
                            data: {
                                state: 'India',
                                flag: 's'
                            },
                            success: function (state) {
                                var responseObject = JSON.parse(state);
                                $('#kycstateEmudra').html('<option value="">Select State</option>'); // Clear existing options
                                $.each(responseObject, function (index, value) {
                                    $('#kycstateEmudra').append('<option value="' + value + '">' + value + '</option>');
                                });
                            }
                        });
                    } else {
                        $("#emudracountry").html('<option value="">Select Country</option><option value="India">India</option><option value="OutsideIndia" selected>Outside India</option>');
                        $('#kycstateEmudra').html('<option value="">Select State</option><option value="Others">Others</option>');
                    }
                } else {
                    $("#emudracountry").html('<option value=' + responseObject.country + ' selected>' + responseObject.country + '</option>');
                    $("#kycstateEmudra").html('<option value=' + responseObject.state + ' selected>' + responseObject.state + '</option>');
                    $("#countrydata").hide();
                    $("#statedata").hide();
                }


            }
        });
        });
        });
        });
        });
    }








     function submitEmudradata() {
        // Debug: Check if #panEmudraValid exists and is visible
        // alert('submit emudra');
        $('.error-message').remove();
        $('.success-message').remove();
        $('.error-coupon').removeClass('error-coupon');
        $('.success-coupon').removeClass('success-coupon');
        var pan = $("#panEmudraValid").val();
        var dob = $("#dobEmudra").val();
        var country = $("#emudracountry").val();
        var state = $("#kycstateEmudra").val();
        var regex = /[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]{1}$/;

        // alert(pan);
        // alert(dob);
        // alert('country'+country);
        // alert('state'+state);

        if (pan == '') {
            $("#panEmudraValid").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#emudraPanValidationMessage").after('<span class="error-message">Enter PAN Number</span>');
        } else if (!regex.test(pan)) {
            $("#panEmudraValid").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#emudraPanValidationMessage").after('<span class="error-message">Invalid PAN Number</span>');
        } else if (country == '') {
            $("#emudracountry").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#validationcountry").after('<span class="error-message">Please select a country</span>');
        } else if (state == '') {
            $("#kycstateEmudra").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#validationstate").after('<span class="error-message">Please select a state</span>');
        } else {
            $("#kycsub").hide();
            $("#loaderkyc").show();
            $("#loaderkyc-overlay").css('display','flex'); // Show overlay and block screen
            var jsonString = JSON.stringify({
                pan: $("#panEmudraValid").val(),
                name: $("#panname").val(),
                dob: $("#dobEmudra").val(),
                userid: "",
                country: $("#emudracountry").val(),
                state: $("#kycstateEmudra").val(),
                flag: 'submitdata',
            });

            console.log('jsonString----------------');
            console.log(jsonString);

            $.ajax({
            type: 'POST',
            url: 'https://www.research360.in/ajax/mo-advice/encryption-data.php',
            data: {jsonString: jsonString},
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function (encrypted) {
            console.log('Encryption response:', encrypted);
                var encryptedData = encrypted['encrypted'];
                $.ajax({
                    url: 'https://www.research360.in/common/emudra/ajax/emudraApiHandler.php',
                    type: 'POST',
                    data: { param: encryptedData, flag: 'submitdata' },
                    success: function (responce) {
                        console.log('Response');
                        console.log(responce)
                        $("#loaderkyc").hide();
                        $("#loaderkyc-overlay").hide(); // Hide overlay and allow screen interaction
                        $("#kycsub").show();
                        var responseObject = JSON.parse(responce);
                        console.log('response-'+responseObject);
                        if (responseObject != null) {
                            if(responseObject.status != "SUCCESS"){
                                $("#errormsgkyc").html(responseObject.message);
                                $('#errorkycpopup').modal('show');
                                $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                            }else{
                            
                                if (responseObject.seeding_status == 'N') {
                                    $("#errormsgkyc").html(responseObject.message);
                                    $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                                    $('#errorkycpopup').modal('show');
                                } else if (responseObject.name == 'N') {
                                        $("#nameEmudra").addClass('error-coupon');
                                        $("#nameEmudra").val('');
                                        $("#kycname").show();
                                    } else if (responseObject.dob == "N") {
                                        $("#errormsgkyc").html(responseObject.message);
                                        $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                                        $('#errorkycpopup').modal('show');
                                    }else if(responseObject.krastatus != "Y"){
                                        $('#kranotcompliant').modal('show');
                                        $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                                    }else {
                                        console.log('responseObject.emudraurl'+responseObject.emudraurl);
                                        // Custom logic for emudraurl
                                        if (responseObject.emudraurl && responseObject.emudraurl !== "") {
                                            console.log('not empty emudraurl');
                                            emudraRedirectUrl = responseObject.emudraurl;
                                            $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                                            setTimeout(function() {
                                                removeModalBackdrop();
                                                showKraCompleteModal();
                                            }, 500);
                                        } else {
                                            console.log('empty emudraurl');
                                            window.location.href = responseObject.emudraurl;
                                            $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                                            removeModalBackdrop();
                                        }
                                    }
                            
                            }
                        } else {
                            console.log('responseObject is null');
                            $("#errormsgkyc").html('Something Went Wrong !');
                            $('#eMudraProcess').modal('hide');
                            $('#errorkycpopup').modal('show');
                        }
                    },
                    error: function() {
                        $("#loaderkyc").hide();
                        $("#loaderkyc-overlay").hide(); // Hide overlay on AJAX error
                        $("#kycsub").show();
                        alert('An error occurred. Please try again.');
                    }
                });
            }
            });
        }
    }

    function validateEmudraPanDob() {
        var isValid = true;
        var pan = $("#panEmudraValid").val();
        var dob = $("#dobEmudra").val();
        var regex = /[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]{1}$/;
        // Remove previous errors
        $("#panEmudraValid").removeClass('error-coupon');
        $("#dobEmudra").removeClass('error-coupon');
        // $("#emudraPanValidationMessage").html("").css('border', '2px solid red'); // Debug border
        $("#dobEmudra").next('.error-message').remove();
        // PAN validation
        if (pan === '') {
            console.log('Setting PAN error: empty');
            $("#panEmudraValid").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#emudraPanValidationMessage").html('<span class="error-message">Enter PAN Number</span>');
            isValid = false;
        } else if (!regex.test(pan)) {
            console.log('Setting PAN error: invalid');
            $("#panEmudraValid").addClass('error-coupon');
            $("#emudraPanValidationMessage").html('<span class="error-message">Invalid PAN Number</span>');
            isValid = false;
        }
        // DOB validation
        if (dob === '' || dob === undefined) {
            $("#dobEmudra").addClass('error-coupon');
            if (!$('#dobEmudra').next('.error-message').length) {
                $("#dobEmudra").after('<span class="error-message">Enter Date of Birth</span>');
            }
            isValid = false;
        }
        return isValid;
    }
    // Patch submitEmudradata to use the new validation
    var originalSubmitEmudradata = submitEmudradata;
    submitEmudradata = function() {
        if (!validateEmudraPanDob()) return;
        originalSubmitEmudradata();
    }

    // Attach click handler for Proceed button in kraCompleteModal
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        var proceedBtn = document.querySelector('#kraCompleteModal .proceedemudra');
        if (proceedBtn) {
            proceedBtn.removeEventListener('click', proceedHandler);
            proceedBtn.addEventListener('click', proceedHandler);
        }
        function proceedHandler() {
            document.getElementById('kraCompleteModal').style.display = 'none';
            removeModalBackdrop();
            if (typeof emudraRedirectUrl !== 'undefined' && emudraRedirectUrl) {
                window.location.href = emudraRedirectUrl;
            }
        }
    });


    // KYC response redirect logic
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        var url = window.location.href;
        if (url.indexOf('status=success') !== -1) {
            setTimeout(function() {
                showEmudraCompleted();
            }, 500);
        } else if (url.indexOf('status=fail') !== -1) {
            var failedModal = document.getElementById('emudraFailed');
            if (failedModal && typeof bootstrap !== 'undefined') {
                var modal = new bootstrap.Modal(failedModal);
                modal.show();
            }
        }
    });
</script>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

 
</body>

                <section class="pb-2 mb-3">
                    <h2 class="mb-3 mt-3">What is the Nifty 50 Option Chain?</h2>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">The Nifty 50 option chain is a list of option contracts with the Nifty 50 index as the underlying asset available for trading in the stock market. It includes both call and put option contracts with different strike prices and expiration dates.</p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">The option chain provides comprehensive data such as options premium, volume, open interest and implied volatility (IV) for every available contract. The Nifty 50 option chain is often used by investors to gauge market sentiment, determine trading activity and even predict future movements. </p>
                </section>
                <section class="pb-2 mb-3">
                    <h2 class="mb-3 mt-3">How To Use The Nifty 50 Option Chain Table?</h2>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">The Nifty 50 option chain table is a powerful tool for traders and investors to analyse and make well-informed decisions. Here is how you can use the live Nifty option chain data effectively.</p>

                    <p class="mb-1 pb-1"><span class="d-inline-flex bg-dark rounded-circle me-2" style="width: 8px;height: 8px;"></span><b>Understand the layout of the table</b></p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">For option chain analysis to be effective, you must first familiarise yourself with the layout of the option chain table. The Nifty 50 option chain table is divided into two segments, with call options on the left and put options on the right. In the centre of the table are the strike prices. Each row on the table represents a specific strike price, with corresponding data for both call and put options. </p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">Each row has multiple columns on both sides. These columns consist of crucial option chain data, such as the premium, change in the premium in brackets, open interest, implied volatility, and volume.</p>

                    <p class="mb-1 pb-1"><span class="d-inline-flex bg-dark rounded-circle me-2" style="width: 8px;height: 8px;"></span><b>Select the expiration date</b></p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">Option chains display contracts with various expiration dates. Choose the expiration date that aligns with your trading strategy. Near-term options tend to be more liquid and have tighter bid-ask spreads, whereas longer-term options contracts offer more time for your strategy to play out.</p>

                    <p class="mb-1 pb-1"><span class="d-inline-flex bg-dark rounded-circle me-2" style="width: 8px;height: 8px;"></span><b>Focus on the volume and open interest data</b></p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">Volume indicates the number of contracts traded during the current session. Open interest, meanwhile, represents the total number of outstanding contracts that have not yet been settled. When reading the live nifty option chain, look for strike prices with higher volume and open interest, as these are often more actively traded and reflect market sentiment better.</p>

                    <p class="mb-1 pb-1"><span class="d-inline-flex bg-dark rounded-circle me-2" style="width: 8px;height: 8px;"></span><b>Analyse the bid and ask prices for the options contracts</b></p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">A bid represents the highest price a buyer is willing to pay, and an ask represents the lowest price a seller is willing to accept. The difference between the two is the bid-ask spread. When conducting option chain analysis, pay attention to these spreads when selecting options to trade, as they can significantly impact your overall profitability.</p>

                    <p class="mb-1 pb-1"><span class="d-inline-flex bg-dark rounded-circle me-2" style="width: 8px;height: 8px;"></span><b>Use Options Greeks</b></p>
                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">Options Greeks provide valuable insights into how option prices may change in response to various factors. By understanding and utilising these Greeks, you can make effective trading decisions about option selection, position sizing, and risk management.</p>

                                            <p class="mb-1 pb-1">The Nifty 50 option chain works by providing a comprehensive overview of all the available call and put option contracts with the Nifty 50 index as the underlying asset. The option chain data is updated on a real-time basis and consists of the premium, open interest, implied volatility, and trading volume for all the option contracts that are available for trading.</p>
                                            <p>Traders can use this information to assess market sentiment, identify potential trading opportunities, and make informed decisions about option trades based on their strategies and risk tolerance.</p>

                                    <div id="Niftysix" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>The lot size of Nifty 50 option contracts is 25. This essentially means that one lot of Nifty 50 options represents 50 units of the underlying Nifty 50 index. Traders can purchase or sell the units of the index in multiples of 25. The maximum lot limit per order for Nifty 50 options is 72, meaning that you cannot purchase or sell more than 1,800 units (25 units x 72 lots) of the Nifty 50 index in a single order.</p>

                    <p class="mb-2 pb-1">An option chain is a list of all available options for a specific stock or asset.                     <h2 class="mb-3 mt-3">How To Use The Option Chain Table?</h2>

                        <li class="py-2"><b>Understanding the Table Structure:</b> The option chain table is typically organised into two sections - calls and puts.                                         <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            When analysing the Option Chain, there are several key elements you should check to make informed decisions. The Option Chain is a table that displays all available options contracts for a specific underlying asset, organised by expiration date and strike price. By checking elements such as Expiration Dates, Strike Prices and Implied Volatility in the Option Chain, you can gain valuable insights into the available options and make more informed decisions when trading or investing in options.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeThree"
                                            aria-expanded="true">
                                            What are the basics of Option Chain Analysis?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeThree" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Option Chain Analysis is a fundamental process used by traders and investors to evaluate and make informed decisions about options contracts. It involves examining the data presented in the Option Chain historical data for a particular underlying asset. Remember that Option Chain Analysis is a skill that improves with experience and understanding of the options market. It's essential to combine this analysis with other technical and fundamental indicators to make well-informed trading decisions.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeFour"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            Why is volume important in the Option Chain?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeFour" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Volume is an essential factor in the Option Chain because it provides valuable insights into the level of activity and liquidity of a particular options contract. It represents the number of option contracts that have been traded on a specific day or during a given time period. Overall, monitoring volume in the Option Chain helps traders identify liquid options with active participation, confirm price movements, and make well-informed trading decisions. It is crucial to pay attention to volume, especially when planning to enter or exit positions, as it can significantly impact the efficiency and cost-effectiveness of your options trading strategies.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeFive"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            How many days can we hold options?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeFive" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Options contracts for individual stocks have a maximum duration of three months. This means you can hold an options contract for a specific stock for a maximum period of three months from the date of its expiration. In other words, options in India have three monthly expiry cycles. It's essential to note that while individual stock options have a maximum duration of three months, index options like Nifty and Bank Nifty have weekly, monthly, and quarterly expiry contracts.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeSix"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            Do options have expiry?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeSix" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Yes, options have an expiration date, which is the last day on which the option can be exercised. After the expiration date passes, the option becomes null and void, and it ceases to have any value. In other words, the option contract expires, and the rights granted by the option are no longer valid.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeSeven"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            Can we sell options anytime?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeSeven" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            It's essential to note that the liquidity and availability of options contracts can vary depending on the underlying asset and the specific strike prices and expiration dates. Additionally, trading hours for options are generally limited to regular market hours on trading days, and options cannot be traded during extended trading hours.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeEight"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            How long are options contracts?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeEight" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Options contracts in India typically have a maximum duration of three months. This means options for individual stocks in India have a maximum expiration period of three months from the date of their introduction. For example, if the current month is September, the near-month contract would expire in September, the next-month contract would expire in October, and the far-month contract would expire in November.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeNine"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            How do you find a trend in the Options Chain?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeNine" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>
                                            Finding a trend in the Options Chain historical data involves analysing the data presented in the Option Chain to identify patterns or directional movements that may indicate market sentiment or potential price movements for the underlying asset. Remember that options trading involves risks, and identifying trends in the Options Chain is not foolproof. It's essential to combine Options Chain analysis with other market indicators and conduct thorough research before making any trading decisions.
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeten"
                                            aria-expanded="false">
                                            What is the meaning of the call and put option?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeten" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>Call and put options are derivative contracts that derive their value from an underlying asset. The underlying asset can be a stock, an index or a commodity. Call options provide the purchaser with the right, but not the obligation, to purchase the underlying asset at a predetermined price on the contract expiration date. Put options, meanwhile, provide the purchaser with the right, but not the obligation, to sell the underlying asset at a predetermined price on the contract expiration date.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#superstarInvestorWireframeeleven"
                                            aria-expanded="false">What is the meaning of open Interest in the option chain?
                                        </button>
                                        
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="superstarInvestorWireframeeleven" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>Open interest in the option chain represents the total number of outstanding unsettled options contracts. You can find the open interest for both calls and puts for every possible strike price and expiration date in an option chain. Open interest can be used to determine the level of trading activity and predict future price movements.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#optionone"
                                            aria-expanded="false">What is a naked option strategy?
                                        </button>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="optionone" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>A naked options strategy involves selling either a call or a put option without possessing the underlying asset. For a naked call to be profitable, the underlying asset’s price must be below the strike price on the expiration date. Meanwhile, for a naked put to be profitable, the underlying asset’s price must be above the strike price on the expiration date. That said, if the market moves unfavourably, the potential for loss with such a strategy is unlimited, making it more suitable for experienced options traders.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#optionsecond"
                                            aria-expanded="false">What is a strangle strategy in options?
                                        </button>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="optionsecond" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>A strangle is an options trading strategy that involves purchasing or selling both a call option with a higher strike price and a put option with a lower strike price. Both options must have the same underlying asset and the same expiration date. The strangle strategy is used when you expect a major move in an asset but are unsure of its direction.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#optionthree"
                                            aria-expanded="false">What is an in-the-money option?
                                        </button>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="optionthree" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>An option contract with a favourable strike price compared to the current market price of the underlying asset is termed an in-the-money or ITM option. For a call option to be ITM, the strike price of the option must be below the market price of the underlying asset. For a put option to be ITM, the strike price of the option must be above the market price of the underlying asset.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="accordion-item border mb-3 pb-0">
                                    <h3 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button
                                            class="accordion-button collapsed bg-white shadow-none font-small font-wt-medium font-colordefault"
                                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#optionfour"
                                            aria-expanded="false">What is an out-of-the-money option?
                                        </button>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div id="optionfour" class="accordion-collapse collapse"
                                        data-bs-parent="#accordionstockmarket4">
                                        <div class="accordion-body pt-0 font-dark-50">
                                            <p>An option contract with an unfavourable strike price compared to the current market price of the underlying asset is termed an out-of-the-money or OTM option. For a call option to be OTM, the strike price of the option must be above the market price of the underlying asset. For a put option to be OTM, the strike price of the option must be below the market price of the underlying asset.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </section>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </section>`;
            }
            $('#contnfaq').html(html);
        }
        function matchValue_(name) {
            let flg = {};
            $.each(FnoStocks, function(key, value) {
                findeData = '';
                findeData = value.stockName;
                if (name.toUpperCase().trim() == findeData.toUpperCase().trim()) {
                    flg = value;
                    // console.log(value, 'Match');
                    return false;
                }
            });
            return flg;
        }
        const splitUrl = getSplitUrl();

        if (splitUrl !== '' && splitUrl !== 'option-chain') {
            const findData = matchValue_(splitUrl.replace(/-/g, ' '));
            
            if (Object.keys(findData).length !== 0) {
                const fnoStockElement = document.getElementById("fno_stock");
                if (fnoStockElement) {
                    fnoStockElement.value = findData.NSEcode;
                    fnoStockElement.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                }
            }
        }

        document.getElementById("fno_stock").addEventListener("click", function () {
            var selectElement = document.getElementById("fno_stock");
            var indexUrl = selectElement.options[selectElement.selectedIndex].text;

            if (indexUrl === "NIFTY50") {
                faq("Niftydiv");
                document.querySelector(".nifty50data").style.display = "block";
                document.querySelector(".optionchaindata").style.display = "none";
            } else {
                faq("optiondiv");
                document.querySelector(".optionchaindata").style.display = "block";
            }

            reloadUrl("future-and-options/option-chain/" + indexUrl.replace(/ /g, "-").toLowerCase());
        });

        window.addEventListener("load", function() {
            document.getElementById("fno_expiry").dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
        });


        document.getElementById("fno_stock").addEventListener("change", function () {
            const symbol = this.value;    
            fetch(`${base_url_web}fno/option/ajax/optionChainExp.php?table_flag=optionChain&symbol=${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}`)
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    console.log(data);
                    document.getElementById("fno_expiry").innerHTML = data.data;
                    document.getElementById("fno_expiry").dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
                })
                .catch(error => console.error("Error:", error));
        });


        Highcharts.setOptions({
            time: {

              lang: {
                      decimalPoint: '.',
                      thousandsSep: ''
                  },
              style: {

              }
        });

        $(document).ready(function(){

            $( "#fno_expiry,#showall,#showallnew" ).change(function() {

                let selectElement = document.getElementById("fno_expiry");
                let firstOption = selectElement.options[0]; // Get the first option
                let selectedText = firstOption.text; // Get the text of the first option
                $(".ocdate").html(selectedText);

                var fno_stock =  $('#fno_stock').val();
                if(fno_stock == 'NIFTY50'){
                    faq('Niftydiv');
                    $(".nifty50data").show();
                    $(".optionchaindata").hide();
                }else{
                    faq('optiondiv');
                    $(".optionchaindata").show();

                    $(".nifty50data").html(fno_stock);
                    $(".nifty50data").show();
                }
                var fno_expiry =  $('#fno_expiry').val();
                $(".loader1").show();
                
                $.post("https://www.research360.in/fno/option/ajax/optionChainApi.php",{
                    stock: fno_stock,
                    expiry: fno_expiry,showall:$('#showall:checked').val(),showallnew:$('#showallnew:checked').val()
                },
                function(html, status){
                    $( "#oichaintable" ).html( html.data);
                    $("span.f_lot_size").html(html.lot_size); 
                    $("div.f_fut_spotprice").html(html.spot_price);
                    $(".fno_pcr").html(html.pcr);
                    $("#content_mob").html(html.datam);
                    $("div.f_fut_price,span.f_fut_price").html(html.future_price);
                    $(".f_fut_maxpain").html(html.max_pain);            
                    api_data = html;
                    chartOptions1a.xAxis.categories=api_data.datahc.map(x=> x[0]);
                    chartOptions1a.series[0].data=api_data.datahc.map(x=> x[1]);
                    chartOptions1a.series[1].data=api_data.datahc.map(x=> x[2]);
                    chartOptions1a.series[3].data=api_data.datahc.map(x=> x[4]);
                    chartOptions1a.series[4].data=api_data.datahc.map(item=> item[5]);
                    chartOptions1a.xAxis.plotLines[0].label.text = 'Strike Price ' + api_data.atm;
                    chartOptions1a.xAxis.plotLines[0].value = api_data.spotstrikepos    ;
                    chartOptions1a.yAxis[2].min= -(api_data.maxoic);
                    chartOptions1a.yAxis[2].max= (api_data.maxoic);
                    Highcharts.chart('chartOptions1', chartOptions1a);  
                    $(".loader1").css('display', 'none');
                }, "json");
            });
        });
        
        

    });
    });
    });
    });

</script>
